The review gate's transient errors (new, optional; DECISIONS-43; `tasks/todo.md` F57): `review-gate`'s run on #1467's ready marking failed on one HTTP 503 from GitHub's API, because `backend/scripts/review_gate.py`'s `_github()` helper retries no 5xx response; the chief's one re-run passed. A required check that runs only on pull-request events and review requests holds a merge until someone re-runs it, by hand or with a fresh review request. A fix (a bounded retry of GitHub's 5xx and network errors inside the gate's 20-minute wait, still failing at once on a 4xx and never passing without a completed Codex review of the head or a recorded override, with unit tests) is a gate change in its own PR, whose merge deploys the backend (routine tier by AGENTS.md section 5's table; the chief would review it at high, since the gate guards every merge).
